## 03_Assignment3/cs231n/layers.py

[源文件](03_Assignment3/cs231n/layers.py)

```python
import numpy as np


# 中文数据流：输入 x 的首维是样本数 N，其余维展平为 D；计算 (N,D)@(D,M)+(M,) 得到 (N,M)。缓存原 x、w、b，供反向恢复输入形状。
def affine_forward(x, w, b):
    """
    Computes the forward pass for an affine (fully-connected) layer.

    The input x has shape (N, d_1, ..., d_k) where x[i] is the ith input.
    We multiply this against a weight matrix of shape (D, M) where
    D = \prod_i d_i

    Inputs:
    x - Input data, of shape (N, d_1, ..., d_k)
    w - Weights, of shape (D, M)
    b - Biases, of shape (M,)

    Returns a tuple of:
    - out: output, of shape (N, M)
    - cache: (x, w, b)
    """
    out = x.reshape(x.shape[0], -1).dot(w) + b
    cache = (x, w, b)
    return out, cache


# 中文数据流：输入为 dout(N,M) 和前向缓存。通过 dout@w.T 得到输入梯度并恢复原形状；展平输入转置乘 dout 得到 dW，沿样本轴求和得到 db。
def affine_backward(dout, cache):
    """
    Computes the backward pass for an affine layer.

    Inputs:
    - dout: Upstream derivative, of shape (N, M)
    - cache: Tuple of:
      - x: Input data, of shape (N, d_1, ... d_k)
      - w: Weights, of shape (D, M)

    Returns a tuple of:
    - dx: Gradient with respect to x, of shape (N, d1, ..., d_k)
    - dw: Gradient with respect to w, of shape (D, M)
    - db: Gradient with respect to b, of shape (M,)
    """
    x, w, b = cache
    dx = dout.dot(w.T).reshape(x.shape)
    dw = x.reshape(x.shape[0], -1).T.dot(dout)
    db = np.sum(dout, axis=0)
    return dx, dw, db


# 中文数据流：逐元素保留正输入、把非正输入置零，输出 shape 不变。缓存输入供反向判断激活区间；该函数没有可训练参数。
def relu_forward(x):
    """
    Computes the forward pass for a layer of rectified linear units (ReLUs).

    Input:
    - x: Inputs, of any shape

    Returns a tuple of:
    - out: Output, of the same shape as x
    - cache: x
    """
    out = np.maximum(0, x)
    cache = x
    return out, cache


# 中文数据流：将初始版本梯度与前向 x>0 掩码相乘。负区间导数为零；零点采用实现约定，不用于证明处处可微。
def relu_backward(dout, cache):
    """
    Computes the backward pass for a layer of rectified linear units (ReLUs).

    Input:
    - dout: Upstream derivatives, of any shape
    - cache: Input x, of same shape as dout

    Returns:
    - dx: Gradient with respect to x
    """
    x = cache
    dx = np.where(x > 0, dout, 0)
    return dx


# 中文数据流：输入 (N,D) 特征与 D 维 gamma/beta。训练时按列计算均值、方差并更新滑动统计，测试时只使用滑动统计；输出与输入同形状，cache 用于手写反向。
def batchnorm_forward(x, gamma, beta, bn_param):
    """
    Forward pass for batch normalization.

    During training the sample mean and (uncorrected) sample variance are
    computed from minibatch statistics and used to normalize the incoming data.
    During training we also keep an exponentially decaying running mean of the mean
    and variance of each feature, and these averages are used to normalize data
    at test-time.

    At each timestep we update the running averages for mean and variance using
    an exponential decay based on the momentum parameter:

    running_mean = momentum * running_mean + (1 - momentum) * sample_mean
    running_var = momentum * running_var + (1 - momentum) * sample_var

    Note that the batch normalization paper suggests a different test-time
    behavior: they compute sample mean and variance for each feature using a
    large number of training images rather than using a running average. For
    this implementation we have chosen to use running averages instead since
    they do not require an additional estimation step; the torch7 implementation
    of batch normalization also uses running averages.

    Input:
    - x: Data of shape (N, D)
    - gamma: Scale parameter of shape (D,)
    - beta: Shift paremeter of shape (D,)
    - bn_param: Dictionary with the following keys:
      - mode: 'train' or 'test'; required
      - eps: Constant for numeric stability
      - momentum: Constant for running mean / variance.
      - running_mean: Array of shape (D,) giving running mean of features
      - running_var Array of shape (D,) giving running variance of features

    Returns a tuple of:
    - out: of shape (N, D)
    - cache: A tuple of values needed in the backward pass
    """
    mode = bn_param['mode']
    eps = bn_param.get('eps', 1e-5)
    momentum = bn_param.get('momentum', 0.9)

    N, D = x.shape
    running_mean = bn_param.get('running_mean', np.zeros(D, dtype=x.dtype))
    running_var = bn_param.get('running_var', np.zeros(D, dtype=x.dtype))

    out, cache = None, None
    if mode == 'train':
        # Compute output
        mu = x.mean(axis=0)
        xc = x - mu
        var = np.mean(xc ** 2, axis=0)
        std = np.sqrt(var + eps)
        xn = xc / std
        out = gamma * xn + beta

        cache = (mode, x, gamma, xc, std, xn, out)

        # Update running average of mean
        running_mean *= momentum
        running_mean += (1 - momentum) * mu

        # Update running average of variance
        running_var *= momentum
        running_var += (1 - momentum) * var
    elif mode == 'test':
        # Using running mean and variance to normalize
        std = np.sqrt(running_var + eps)
        xn = (x - running_mean) / std
        out = gamma * xn + beta
        cache = (mode, x, xn, gamma, beta, std)
    else:
        raise ValueError('Invalid forward batchnorm mode "%s"' % mode)

    # Store the updated running means back into bn_param
    bn_param['running_mean'] = running_mean
    bn_param['running_var'] = running_var

    return out, cache


# 中文数据流：从 dout 依次传播到缩放、标准化、方差和均值分支。共享统计使同一列的所有样本相互关联；返回 dx(N,D)、dgamma(D,) 和 dbeta(D,)。
def batchnorm_backward(dout, cache):
    """
    Backward pass for batch normalization.

    For this implementation, you should write out a computation graph for
    batch normalization on paper and propagate gradients backward through
    intermediate nodes.

    Inputs:
    - dout: Upstream derivatives, of shape (N, D)
    - cache: Variable of intermediates from batchnorm_forward.

    Returns a tuple of:
    - dx: Gradient with respect to inputs x, of shape (N, D)
    - dgamma: Gradient with respect to scale parameter gamma, of shape (D,)
    - dbeta: Gradient with respect to shift parameter beta, of shape (D,)
    """
    mode = cache[0]
    if mode == 'train':
        mode, x, gamma, xc, std, xn, out = cache

        N = x.shape[0]
        dbeta = dout.sum(axis=0)
        dgamma = np.sum(xn * dout, axis=0)
        dxn = gamma * dout
        dxc = dxn / std
        dstd = -np.sum((dxn * xc) / (std * std), axis=0)
        dvar = 0.5 * dstd / std
        dxc += (2.0 / N) * xc * dvar
        dmu = np.sum(dxc, axis=0)
        dx = dxc - dmu / N
    elif mode == 'test':
        mode, x, xn, gamma, beta, std = cache
        dbeta = dout.sum(axis=0)
        dgamma = np.sum(xn * dout, axis=0)
        dxn = gamma * dout
        dx = dxn / std
    else:
        raise ValueError(mode)

    return dx, dgamma, dbeta


# 中文数据流：把 NCHW 转为 (N·H·W,C)，复用二维 BN 对每个通道归一化，再转回 NCHW。gamma/beta 长度是 C，而不是 H·W。
def spatial_batchnorm_forward(x, gamma, beta, bn_param):
    """
    Computes the forward pass for spatial batch normalization.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input data of shape (N, C, H, W)
    - gamma: Scale parameter, of shape (C,)
    - beta: Shift parameter, of shape (C,)
    - bn_param: Dictionary with the following keys:
      - mode: 'train' or 'test'; required
      - eps: Constant for numeric stability
      - momentum: Constant for running mean / variance. momentum=0 means that
        old information is discarded completely at every time step, while
        momentum=1 means that new information is never incorporated. The
        default of momentum=0.9 should work well in most situations.
      - running_mean: Array of shape (D,) giving running mean of features
      - running_var Array of shape (D,) giving running variance of features

    Returns a tuple of:
    - out: Output data, of shape (N, C, H, W)
    - cache: Values needed for the backward pass
    """
    N, C, H, W = x.shape
    x_flat = x.transpose(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(-1, C)
    out_flat, cache = batchnorm_forward(x_flat, gamma, beta, bn_param)
    out = out_flat.reshape(N, H, W, C).transpose(0, 3, 1, 2)
    return out, cache


# 中文数据流：先把初始版本 NCHW 梯度转换为二维 BN 布局，执行反向，再恢复原图像布局。输出 dx 和按通道聚合的 dgamma、dbeta。
def spatial_batchnorm_backward(dout, cache):
    """
    Computes the backward pass for spatial batch normalization.

    Inputs:
    - dout: Upstream derivatives, of shape (N, C, H, W)
    - cache: Values from the forward pass

    Returns a tuple of:
    - dx: Gradient with respect to inputs, of shape (N, C, H, W)
    - dgamma: Gradient with respect to scale parameter, of shape (C,)
    - dbeta: Gradient with respect to shift parameter, of shape (C,)
    """
    N, C, H, W = dout.shape
    dout_flat = dout.transpose(0, 2, 3, 1).reshape(-1, C)
    dx_flat, dgamma, dbeta = batchnorm_backward(dout_flat, cache)
    dx = dx_flat.reshape(N, H, W, C).transpose(0, 3, 1, 2)
    return dx, dgamma, dbeta


# 中文数据流：输入得分 (N,C) 与标签 (N,)。错误类别 margin=max(0,s_j−s_y+1)，正确类别清零；返回平均损失及对得分的梯度。
def svm_loss(x, y):
    """
    Computes the loss and gradient using for multiclass SVM classification.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input data, of shape (N, C) where x[i, j] is the score for the jth class
      for the ith input.
    - y: Vector of labels, of shape (N,) where y[i] is the label for x[i] and
      0 <= y[i] < C

    Returns a tuple of:
    - loss: Scalar giving the loss
    - dx: Gradient of the loss with respect to x
    """
    N = x.shape[0]
    correct_class_scores = x[np.arange(N), y]
    margins = np.maximum(0, x - correct_class_scores[:, np.newaxis] + 1.0)
    margins[np.arange(N), y] = 0
    loss = np.sum(margins) / N
    num_pos = np.sum(margins > 0, axis=1)
    dx = np.zeros_like(x)
    dx[margins > 0] = 1
    dx[np.arange(N), y] -= num_pos
    dx /= N
    return loss, dx


# 中文数据流：先逐行减最大 logit，再计算 log-sum-exp 与正确类别交叉熵。得分梯度为 (概率−one_hot)/N，输出标量损失和 (N,C) 梯度。
def softmax_loss(x, y):
    """
    Computes the loss and gradient for softmax classification.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input data, of shape (N, C) where x[i, j] is the score for the jth class
      for the ith input.
    - y: Vector of labels, of shape (N,) where y[i] is the label for x[i] and
      0 <= y[i] < C

    Returns a tuple of:
    - loss: Scalar giving the loss
    - dx: Gradient of the loss with respect to x
    """
    probs = np.exp(x - np.max(x, axis=1, keepdims=True))
    probs /= np.sum(probs, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    N = x.shape[0]
    loss = -np.sum(np.log(probs[np.arange(N), y])) / N
    dx = probs.copy()
    dx[np.arange(N), y] -= 1
    dx /= N
    return loss, dx
```

## 03_Assignment3/cs231n/optim.py

[源文件](03_Assignment3/cs231n/optim.py)

```python
import numpy as np

"""
This file implements various first-order update rules that are commonly used for
training neural networks. Each update rule accepts current weights and the
gradient of the loss with respect to those weights and produces the next set of
weights. Each update rule has the same interface:

def update(w, dw, config=None):

Inputs:
  - w: A numpy array giving the current weights.
  - dw: A numpy array of the same shape as w giving the gradient of the
    loss with respect to w.
  - config: A dictionary containing hyperparameter values such as learning rate,
    momentum, etc. If the update rule requires caching values over many
    iterations, then config will also hold these cached values.

Returns:
  - next_w: The next point after the update.
  - config: The config dictionary to be passed to the next iteration of the
    update rule.

NOTE: For most update rules, the default learning rate will probably not perform
well; however the default values of the other hyperparameters should work well
for a variety of different problems.

For efficiency, update rules may perform in-place updates, mutating w and
setting next_w equal to w.
"""


# 中文数据流：输入当前参数、同形状梯度及学习率，沿负梯度更新。返回新参数和配置，某些实现原地修改输入参数，调用方不能假定原数组仍是旧值。
def sgd(w, dw, config=None):
    """
    Performs vanilla stochastic gradient descent.

    config format:
    - learning_rate: Scalar learning rate.
    """
    if config is None: config = {}
    config.setdefault('learning_rate', 1e-2)

    w -= config['learning_rate'] * dw
    return w, config


# 中文数据流：分别更新一阶矩 m 和二阶矩 v，递增时间步并进行初值偏差校正，再更新参数。Assignment 2 使用标准校正矩写法；Assignment 3 保留课程 alpha/epsilon 位置，GPU 对照也保持同一公式。
def adam(x, dx, config=None):
    """
    Uses the Adam update rule, which incorporates moving averages of both the
    gradient and its square and a bias correction term.

    config format:
    - learning_rate: Scalar learning rate.
    - beta1: Decay rate for moving average of first moment of gradient.
    - beta2: Decay rate for moving average of second moment of gradient.
    - epsilon: Small scalar used for smoothing to avoid dividing by zero.
    - m: Moving average of gradient.
    - v: Moving average of squared gradient.
    - t: Iteration number.
    """
    if config is None: config = {}
    config.setdefault('learning_rate', 1e-3)
    config.setdefault('beta1', 0.9)
    config.setdefault('beta2', 0.999)
    config.setdefault('epsilon', 1e-8)
    config.setdefault('m', np.zeros_like(x))
    config.setdefault('v', np.zeros_like(x))
    config.setdefault('t', 0)

    next_x = None
    beta1, beta2, eps = config['beta1'], config['beta2'], config['epsilon']
    t, m, v = config['t'], config['m'], config['v']
    m = beta1 * m + (1 - beta1) * dx
    v = beta2 * v + (1 - beta2) * (dx * dx)
    t += 1
    alpha = config['learning_rate'] * np.sqrt(1 - beta2 ** t) / (1 - beta1 ** t)
    x -= alpha * (m / (np.sqrt(v) + eps))
    config['t'] = t
    config['m'] = m
    config['v'] = v
    next_x = x

    return next_x, config
```

## 03_Assignment3/cs231n/rnn_layers.py

[源文件](03_Assignment3/cs231n/rnn_layers.py)

```python
from __future__ import print_function, division
from builtins import range
import numpy as np

"""
This file defines layer types that are commonly used for recurrent neural
networks.
"""


# 中文数据流：输入 x(N,D)、prev_h(N,H) 及两组权重，计算 next_h=tanh(xWx+prev_hWh+b)。输出隐藏状态和反向缓存，不在单步函数内修改训练参数。
def rnn_step_forward(x, prev_h, Wx, Wh, b):
    """
    Run the forward pass for a single timestep of a vanilla RNN that uses a tanh
    activation function.

    The input data has dimension D, the hidden state has dimension H, and we use
    a minibatch size of N.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input data for this timestep, of shape (N, D).
    - prev_h: Hidden state from previous timestep, of shape (N, H)
    - Wx: Weight matrix for input-to-hidden connections, of shape (D, H)
    - Wh: Weight matrix for hidden-to-hidden connections, of shape (H, H)
    - b: Biases of shape (H,)

    Returns a tuple of:
    - next_h: Next hidden state, of shape (N, H)
    - cache: Tuple of values needed for the backward pass.
    """
    next_h, cache = None, None
    ##############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement a single forward step for the vanilla RNN. Store the next  #
    # hidden state and any values you need for the backward pass in the next_h   #
    # and cache variables respectively.                                          #
    ##############################################################################
    # 前向传播
    # ht=tanh(Wx@Xt+Wh@ht-1+b)
    next_h = np.tanh(x.dot(Wx) + prev_h.dot(Wh) + b)

    cache = (x, prev_h, Wx, Wh, b, next_h)
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################
    return next_h, cache


# 中文数据流：输入 dnext_h 和单步 cache，先乘 tanh 导数，再分别沿 xWx 与 hWh 回传。返回 dx、dprev_h、dWx、dWh、db，权重梯度在 batch 轴聚合。
def rnn_step_backward(dnext_h, cache):
    """
    Backward pass for a single timestep of a vanilla RNN.

    Inputs:
    - dnext_h: Gradient of loss with respect to next hidden state
    - cache: Cache object from the forward pass

    Returns a tuple of:
    - dx: Gradients of input data, of shape (N, D)
    - dprev_h: Gradients of previous hidden state, of shape (N, H)
    - dWx: Gradients of input-to-hidden weights, of shape (D, H)
    - dWh: Gradients of hidden-to-hidden weights, of shape (H, H)
    - db: Gradients of bias vector, of shape (H,)
    """
    dx, dprev_h, dWx, dWh, db = None, None, None, None, None
    ##############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement the backward pass for a single step of a vanilla RNN.      #
    #                                                                            #
    # HINT: For the tanh function, you can compute the local derivative in terms #
    # of the output value from tanh.                                             #
    ##############################################################################
    x, prev_h, Wx, Wh, b, next_h = cache
    # 反向传播先经过tanh
    dtanh = dnext_h * (1 - next_h ** 2)  # (N,H)
    # 线性项求和即可，注意形状即可
    db = np.sum(dtanh, axis=0)

    dWx = x.T.dot(dtanh)  # (D,N) * (N,H)

    dWh = prev_h.T.dot(dtanh)  # (H,N) * (N,H)

    dprev_h = dtanh.dot(Wh.T)  # (N,H) * (H,H)= (N,H)

    dx = dtanh.dot(Wx.T)  # (N,H)*(H,D)=(N,D)
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################
    return dx, dprev_h, dWx, dWh, db


# 中文数据流：输入完整序列 (N,T,D) 和初始隐藏状态，从 t=0 递推到 T−1。每步共享同一组 Wx、Wh、b，输出所有时刻隐藏状态并按时间保存 cache。
def rnn_forward(x, h0, Wx, Wh, b):
    """
    Run a vanilla RNN forward on an entire sequence of data. We assume an input
    sequence composed of T vectors, each of dimension D. The RNN uses a hidden
    size of H, and we work over a minibatch containing N sequences. After running
    the RNN forward, we return the hidden states for all timesteps.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input data for the entire timeseries, of shape (N, T, D).
    - h0: Initial hidden state, of shape (N, H)
    - Wx: Weight matrix for input-to-hidden connections, of shape (D, H)
    - Wh: Weight matrix for hidden-to-hidden connections, of shape (H, H)
    - b: Biases of shape (H,)

    Returns a tuple of:
    - h: Hidden states for the entire timeseries, of shape (N, T, H).
    - cache: Values needed in the backward pass
    """
    h, cache = None, None
    ##############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement forward pass for a vanilla RNN running on a sequence of    #
    # input data. You should use the rnn_step_forward function that you defined  #
    # above. You can use a for loop to help compute the forward pass.            #
    ##############################################################################
    N, T, D = x.shape
    h = np.zeros((N, T, h0.shape[1]))
    cache = []
    ht = h0
    for t in range(T):
        ht, cache_step = rnn_step_forward(x[:, t, :], ht, Wx, Wh, b)
        h[:, t, :] = ht
        cache.append(cache_step)
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################
    return h, cache


# 中文数据流：从最后时刻往前遍历，把当前输出梯度与未来状态回传梯度相加。各时间步的共享权重梯度累加，最终回传到 h0，并返回完整 dx(N,T,D)。
def rnn_backward(dh, cache):
    """
    Compute the backward pass for a vanilla RNN over an entire sequence of data.

    Inputs:
    - dh: Upstream gradients of all hidden states, of shape (N, T, H)

    Returns a tuple of:
    - dx: Gradient of inputs, of shape (N, T, D)
    - dh0: Gradient of initial hidden state, of shape (N, H)
    - dWx: Gradient of input-to-hidden weights, of shape (D, H)
    - dWh: Gradient of hidden-to-hidden weights, of shape (H, H)
    - db: Gradient of biases, of shape (H,)
    """
    dx, dh0, dWx, dWh, db = None, None, None, None, None
    ##############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement the backward pass for a vanilla RNN running an entire      #
    # sequence of data. You should use the rnn_step_backward function that you   #
    # defined above. You can use a for loop to help compute the backward pass.   #
    ##############################################################################
    N, T, H = dh.shape
    D = cache[0][0].shape[1]
    dx = np.zeros((N, T, D))
    dh0 = np.zeros((N, H))
    dprev_h = np.zeros((N, H))
    dWx = np.zeros((D, H))
    dWh = np.zeros((H, H))
    db = np.zeros((H,))
    #
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # 这里的dout包含两部分从上面过来的dh，还有从右边过来的之前算出来的dpre_h(对于当前就是dnext_h)
        dnext_h = dh[:, t, :] + dprev_h
        dx[:, t, :], dprev_h, dWxt, dWht, dbt = rnn_step_backward(dnext_h, cache.pop())
        # 参数共享累加
        dWx += dWxt
        dWh += dWht
        db += dbt
        # 迭代到最后即为h0
        dh0 = dprev_h
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################
    return dx, dh0, dWx, dWh, db


# 中文数据流：用整数词索引 x(N,T) 从词向量矩阵 W(V,D) 逐位置查表，输出 (N,T,D)。索引本身不可求导，学习对象是被选中的词向量行。
def word_embedding_forward(x, W):
    """
    Forward pass for word embeddings. We operate on minibatches of size N where
    each sequence has length T. We assume a vocabulary of V words, assigning each
    to a vector of dimension D.

    Inputs:
    - x: Integer array of shape (N, T) giving indices of words. Each element idx
      of x muxt be in the range 0 <= idx < V.
    - W: Weight matrix of shape (V, D) giving word vectors for all words.

    Returns a tuple of:
    - out: Array of shape (N, T, D) giving word vectors for all input words.
    - cache: Values needed for the backward pass
    """
    out, cache = None, None
    ##############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement the forward pass for word embeddings.                      #
    #                                                                            #
    # HINT: This can be done in one line using NumPy's array indexing.           #
    ##############################################################################
    out = W[x]
    cache = (x, W)
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################
    return out, cache


# 中文数据流：对重复词索引的初始版本梯度累加到同一 dW 行；使用可处理重复索引的累加方式，避免普通索引赋值丢掉重复贡献。
def word_embedding_backward(dout, cache):
    """
    Backward pass for word embeddings. We cannot back-propagate into the words
    since they are integers, so we only return gradient for the word embedding
    matrix.

    HINT: Look up the function np.add.at

    Inputs:
    - dout: Upstream gradients of shape (N, T, D)
    - cache: Values from the forward pass

    Returns:
    - dW: Gradient of word embedding matrix, of shape (V, D).
    """
    dW = None
    ##############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement the backward pass for word embeddings.                     #
    #                                                                            #
    # Note that Words can appear more than once in a sequence.                   #
    # HINT: Look up the function np.add.at                                       #
    ##############################################################################
    x, W = cache
    N, T, D = dout.shape
    dW = np.zeros(W.shape)
    np.add.at(dW, x.flatten(), dout.reshape(-1, D))  # 按照x的下标给出dout
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################
    return dW


# 中文数据流：按正负输入分别计算 sigmoid，避免 exp 在极大负数或正数处溢出。输入输出形状一致，门控输出范围为 (0,1)。
def sigmoid(x):
    """
    A numerically stable version of the logistic sigmoid function.
    """
    pos_mask = (x >= 0)
    neg_mask = (x < 0)
    z = np.zeros_like(x)
    z[pos_mask] = np.exp(-x[pos_mask])
    z[neg_mask] = np.exp(x[neg_mask])
    top = np.ones_like(x)
    top[neg_mask] = z[neg_mask]
    return top / (1 + z)


# 中文数据流：把线性输出分为 i/f/o/g 四块；前三块 sigmoid，候选记忆 tanh。通过 c_next=f*c_prev+i*g 和 h_next=o*tanh(c_next) 更新，缓存门值和状态供反向。
def lstm_step_forward(x, prev_h, prev_c, Wx, Wh, b):
    """
    Forward pass for a single timestep of an LSTM.

    The input data has dimension D, the hidden state has dimension H, and we use
    a minibatch size of N.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input data, of shape (N, D)
    - prev_h: Previous hidden state, of shape (N, H)
    - prev_c: previous cell state, of shape (N, H)
    - Wx: Input-to-hidden weights, of shape (D, 4H)
    - Wh: Hidden-to-hidden weights, of shape (H, 4H)
    - b: Biases, of shape (4H,)

    Returns a tuple of:
    - next_h: Next hidden state, of shape (N, H)
    - next_c: Next cell state, of shape (N, H)
    - cache: Tuple of values needed for backward pass.
    """
    next_h, next_c, cache = None, None, None
    #############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement the forward pass for a single timestep of an LSTM.        #
    # You may want to use the numerically stable sigmoid implementation above.  #
    #############################################################################
    H = prev_h.shape[1]
    z = x.dot(Wx) + prev_h.dot(Wh) + b
    i = sigmoid(z[:, 0:H])
    f = sigmoid(z[:, H:2 * H])
    o = sigmoid(z[:, 2 * H:3 * H])
    g = np.tanh(z[:, 3 * H:4 * H])
    next_c = f * prev_c + i * g
    next_h = o * np.tanh(next_c)
    cache = (i, f, o, g, next_c, prev_c, prev_h, Wx, Wh, x)
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################

    return next_h, next_c, cache


# 中文数据流：先汇合 h_next 和未来 c_next 对细胞状态的梯度，再分别回传 i/f/o/g。按与前向一致的门顺序拼接 dz，计算输入、前状态和权重梯度。
def lstm_step_backward(dnext_h, dnext_c, cache):
    """
    Backward pass for a single timestep of an LSTM.

    Inputs:
    - dnext_h: Gradients of next hidden state, of shape (N, H)
    - dnext_c: Gradients of next cell state, of shape (N, H)
    - cache: Values from the forward pass

    Returns a tuple of:
    - dx: Gradient of input data, of shape (N, D)
    - dprev_h: Gradient of previous hidden state, of shape (N, H)
    - dprev_c: Gradient of previous cell state, of shape (N, H)
    - dWx: Gradient of input-to-hidden weights, of shape (D, 4H)
    - dWh: Gradient of hidden-to-hidden weights, of shape (H, 4H)
    - db: Gradient of biases, of shape (4H,)
    """
    dx, dh, dc, dWx, dWh, db = None, None, None, None, None, None
    #############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement the backward pass for a single timestep of an LSTM.       #
    #                                                                           #
    # HINT: For sigmoid and tanh you can compute local derivatives in terms of  #
    # the output value from the nonlinearity.                                   #
    #############################################################################
    i, f, o, g, next_c, prev_c, prev_h, Wx, Wh, x = cache
    # o只接收Ct的梯度流
    do = dnext_h * np.tanh(next_c) * o * (1 - o)
    # Ct经过tanh其返回的梯度流记作dtanhc
    dtanh_c = dnext_h * o * (1 - np.tanh(next_c) ** 2)
    # i,f,g，接收dnext_c和dtanh_c两个梯度流
    dnext_out = dnext_c + dtanh_c
    di = dnext_out * g * i * (1 - i)
    df = dnext_out * prev_c * f * (1 - f)
    dg = dnext_out * i * (1 - g ** 2)
    dprev_c = dnext_out * f
    # 把di,df,do,dg连接起来构成dz
    dz = np.hstack((di, df, do, dg))  # (N, 4H)
    # 线性部分Wx * Xt +Wh * ht-1 + b = z
    dWx = x.T.dot(dz)  # (D,N) * (N,4H)
    dWh = prev_h.T.dot(dz)  # (H, N) * (N,4H)
    db = np.sum(dz, axis=0)
    dprev_h = dz.dot(Wh.T)  # (N,4H) * (4H,H) =(N,H)
    dx = dz.dot(Wx.T)  # =(N,4H) * (4H,H) =(N,H)
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################

    return dx, dprev_h, dprev_c, dWx, dWh, db


# 中文数据流：以 h0 和全零 c0 开始逐词更新 LSTM，保存每个时间步的 gate/cache。输出 (N,T,H) 隐藏序列，词表投影在外部统一执行。
def lstm_forward(x, h0, Wx, Wh, b):
    """
    Forward pass for an LSTM over an entire sequence of data. We assume an input
    sequence composed of T vectors, each of dimension D. The LSTM uses a hidden
    size of H, and we work over a minibatch containing N sequences. After running
    the LSTM forward, we return the hidden states for all timesteps.

    Note that the initial cell state is passed as input, but the initial cell
    state is set to zero. Also note that the cell state is not returned; it is
    an internal variable to the LSTM and is not accessed from outside.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input data of shape (N, T, D)
    - h0: Initial hidden state of shape (N, H)
    - Wx: Weights for input-to-hidden connections, of shape (D, 4H)
    - Wh: Weights for hidden-to-hidden connections, of shape (H, 4H)
    - b: Biases of shape (4H,)

    Returns a tuple of:
    - h: Hidden states for all timesteps of all sequences, of shape (N, T, H)
    - cache: Values needed for the backward pass.
    """
    h, cache = None, None
    #############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement the forward pass for an LSTM over an entire timeseries.   #
    # You should use the lstm_step_forward function that you just defined.      #
    #############################################################################
    N, T, D = x.shape
    H = h0.shape[1]
    prev_h = h0
    h = np.zeros((N, T, H))
    prev_c = np.zeros((N, H))
    cache = []
    for t in range(T):
        next_h, next_c, cache_step = lstm_step_forward(x[:, t, :], prev_h, prev_c, Wx, Wh, b)
        h[:, t, :] = next_h
        prev_h = next_h
        prev_c = next_c
        cache.append(cache_step)
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################

    return h, cache


# 中文数据流：逆序传递隐藏与细胞两条状态梯度；共享 Wx、Wh、b 的导数对时间求和。返回 dx、dh0 和三组参数梯度，不能遗漏跨时间的 dnext_c。
def lstm_backward(dh, cache):
    """
    Backward pass for an LSTM over an entire sequence of data.]

    Inputs:
    - dh: Upstream gradients of hidden states, of shape (N, T, H)
    - cache: Values from the forward pass

    Returns a tuple of:
    - dx: Gradient of input data of shape (N, T, D)
    - dh0: Gradient of initial hidden state of shape (N, H)
    - dWx: Gradient of input-to-hidden weight matrix of shape (D, 4H)
    - dWh: Gradient of hidden-to-hidden weight matrix of shape (H, 4H)
    - db: Gradient of biases, of shape (4H,)
    """
    dx, dh0, dWx, dWh, db = None, None, None, None, None
    #############################################################################
    # TODO: Implement the backward pass for an LSTM over an entire timeseries.  #
    # You should use the lstm_step_backward function that you just defined.     #
    #############################################################################
    N, T, H = dh.shape
    _, D = cache[0][-1].shape
    dprev_h = np.zeros((N, H))
    dx = np.zeros((N, T, D))
    dh0 = np.zeros((N, H))
    dWx = np.zeros((D, 4 * H))
    dWh = np.zeros((H, 4 * H))
    db = np.zeros((4 * H,))
    dnext_c = np.zeros((N, H))
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # 和RNN同理，LSTM的dout包含两部分从上面过来的dh，还有从右边过来的之前算出来的dpre_h(对于当前就是dnext_h)
        dnext_h = dh[:, t, :] + dprev_h
        dx[:, t, :], dprev_h, dprev_c, dWxt, dWht, dbt = lstm_step_backward(dnext_h, dnext_c, cache[t])
        # 每次需要迭代Ct
        dnext_c = dprev_c
        # 参数共享
        dWx += dWxt
        dWh += dWht
        db += dbt
        # 迭代到最后即为h0
        dh0 = dprev_h
    ##############################################################################
    #                               END OF YOUR CODE                             #
    ##############################################################################

    return dx, dh0, dWx, dWh, db


# 中文数据流：将 (N,T,D) 展平为 (N·T,D) 后乘共享投影矩阵，恢复 (N,T,M) 并加偏置。所有时间步使用同一个词表映射。
def temporal_affine_forward(x, w, b):
    """
    Forward pass for a temporal affine layer. The input is a set of D-dimensional
    vectors arranged into a minibatch of N timeseries, each of length T. We use
    an affine function to transform each of those vectors into a new vector of
    dimension M.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input data of shape (N, T, D)
    - w: Weights of shape (D, M)
    - b: Biases of shape (M,)

    Returns a tuple of:
    - out: Output data of shape (N, T, M)
    - cache: Values needed for the backward pass
    """
    N, T, D = x.shape
    M = b.shape[0]
    out = x.reshape(N * T, D).dot(w).reshape(N, T, M) + b
    cache = x, w, b, out
    return out, cache


# 中文数据流：把 dout(N,T,M) 展平，对输入与共享权重求导；db 同时沿样本和时间轴求和。输出恢复原序列形状。
def temporal_affine_backward(dout, cache):
    """
    Backward pass for temporal affine layer.

    Input:
    - dout: Upstream gradients of shape (N, T, M)
    - cache: Values from forward pass

    Returns a tuple of:
    - dx: Gradient of input, of shape (N, T, D)
    - dw: Gradient of weights, of shape (D, M)
    - db: Gradient of biases, of shape (M,)
    """
    x, w, b, out = cache
    N, T, D = x.shape
    M = b.shape[0]

    dx = dout.reshape(N * T, M).dot(w.T).reshape(N, T, D)
    dw = dout.reshape(N * T, M).T.dot(x.reshape(N * T, D)).T
    db = dout.sum(axis=(0, 1))

    return dx, dw, db


# 中文数据流：计算每个时间位置的交叉熵，用 mask 屏蔽 NULL 填充，再对 batch 数 N 平均。输出对 logits 的梯度也乘同一 mask，因此填充位置不会更新参数。
def temporal_softmax_loss(x, y, mask, verbose=False):
    """
    A temporal version of softmax loss for use in RNNs. We assume that we are
    making predictions over a vocabulary of size V for each timestep of a
    timeseries of length T, over a minibatch of size N. The input x gives scores
    for all vocabulary elements at all timesteps, and y gives the indices of the
    ground-truth element at each timestep. We use a cross-entropy loss at each
    timestep, summing the loss over all timesteps and averaging across the
    minibatch.

    As an additional complication, we may want to ignore the model output at some
    timesteps, since sequences of different length may have been combined into a
    minibatch and padded with NULL tokens. The optional mask argument tells us
    which elements should contribute to the loss.

    Inputs:
    - x: Input scores, of shape (N, T, V)
    - y: Ground-truth indices, of shape (N, T) where each element is in the range
         0 <= y[i, t] < V
    - mask: Boolean array of shape (N, T) where mask[i, t] tells whether or not
      the scores at x[i, t] should contribute to the loss.

    Returns a tuple of:
    - loss: Scalar giving loss
    - dx: Gradient of loss with respect to scores x.
    """

    N, T, V = x.shape

    x_flat = x.reshape(N * T, V)
    y_flat = y.reshape(N * T)
    mask_flat = mask.reshape(N * T)

    probs = np.exp(x_flat - np.max(x_flat, axis=1, keepdims=True))
    probs /= np.sum(probs, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    loss = -np.sum(mask_flat * np.log(probs[np.arange(N * T), y_flat])) / N
    dx_flat = probs.copy()
    dx_flat[np.arange(N * T), y_flat] -= 1
    dx_flat /= N
    dx_flat *= mask_flat[:, None]

    if verbose: print('dx_flat: ', dx_flat.shape)

    dx = dx_flat.reshape(N, T, V)

    return loss, dx
```

## 03_Assignment3/cs231n/torch_captioning.py

[源文件](03_Assignment3/cs231n/torch_captioning.py)

```python
"""与课程 NumPy RNN/LSTM 对应的 GPU 实现。

NumPy LSTM 门顺序为 i,f,o,g；PyTorch 为 i,f,g,o。导入参数和导出梯度
时都显式换序。第二组循环偏置固定为零，避免增加原模型没有的可训练参数。
"""
import copy, json, os, time
import functools, random
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from nltk.translate.bleu_score import sentence_bleu
from cs231n.classifiers.rnn import CaptioningRNN
from cs231n.coco_utils import decode_captions
from study_runtime import ROOT

def isolated_random_state(function):
    """方法二运行前后恢复外部随机状态，训练内部仍独立保存自身 RNG 检查点。"""
    @functools.wraps(function)
    def wrapped(*args, **kwargs):
        numpy_state=np.random.get_state();python_state=random.getstate()
        try:
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=list(range(torch.cuda.device_count()))):
                return function(*args, **kwargs)
        finally:
            np.random.set_state(numpy_state);random.setstate(python_state)
    return wrapped

def gate_permutation(array):
    """最后一维四块按 0,1,3,2 重排；该置换恰好也是自己的逆。"""
    chunks=np.split(array,4,axis=-1)
    return np.concatenate([chunks[i] for i in (0,1,3,2)],axis=-1)

class TorchCaptioner(nn.Module):
    """图像特征→h0；词嵌入→循环层→词表 logits，与原模型参数逐一对应。"""
    def __init__(self,numpy_model):
        super().__init__()
        p=numpy_model.params
        self.cell_type=numpy_model.cell_type
        self._null=numpy_model._null;self._start=numpy_model._start
        self.word_to_idx=numpy_model.word_to_idx
        for key in ('W_embed','W_proj','b_proj','W_vocab','b_vocab'):
            setattr(self,key,nn.Parameter(torch.from_numpy(p[key].copy())))
        hidden=p['Wh'].shape[0];worddim=p['Wx'].shape[0]
        layer=nn.RNN if self.cell_type=='rnn' else nn.LSTM
        self.recurrent=layer(worddim,hidden,batch_first=True).to(dtype=self.W_embed.dtype)
        change=gate_permutation if self.cell_type=='lstm' else lambda x:x
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.recurrent.weight_ih_l0.copy_(torch.from_numpy(change(p['Wx']).T.copy()))
            self.recurrent.weight_hh_l0.copy_(torch.from_numpy(change(p['Wh']).T.copy()))
            self.recurrent.bias_ih_l0.copy_(torch.from_numpy(change(p['b']).copy()))
            self.recurrent.bias_hh_l0.zero_()
        self.recurrent.bias_hh_l0.requires_grad_(False)

    def forward(self,features,caption_inputs):
        # h0 的 shape 为 (1,N,H)，循环层的输出为 (N,T,H)。
        h0=(features@self.W_proj+self.b_proj).unsqueeze(0)
        initial=h0 if self.cell_type=='rnn' else (h0,torch.zeros_like(h0))
        embedded=F.embedding(caption_inputs,self.W_embed)
        hidden,_=self.recurrent(embedded,initial)
        return hidden@self.W_vocab+self.b_vocab

    def loss(self,features,captions):
        # teacher forcing：输入去掉末词，目标去掉 START；NULL 不计入损失。
        logits=self(features,captions[:,:-1])
        return F.cross_entropy(logits.reshape(-1,logits.shape[-1]),captions[:,1:].reshape(-1),ignore_index=self._null,reduction='sum')/len(captions)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def sample(self,features,max_length=30):
        h0=(features@self.W_proj+self.b_proj).unsqueeze(0)
        state=h0 if self.cell_type=='rnn' else (h0,torch.zeros_like(h0))
        word=torch.full((len(features),),self._start,device=features.device,dtype=torch.long)
        captions=[]
        for _ in range(max_length):
            hidden,state=self.recurrent(F.embedding(word,self.W_embed).unsqueeze(1),state)
            word=(hidden[:,0]@self.W_vocab+self.b_vocab).argmax(dim=-1)
            captions.append(word)
        return torch.stack(captions,dim=1)

    def numpy_gradients(self):
        """把框架梯度映射回课程参数名，供独立的数值一致性验证。"""
        convert=lambda t:t.detach().cpu().numpy()
        result={key:convert(getattr(self,key).grad) for key in ('W_embed','W_proj','b_proj','W_vocab','b_vocab')}
        change=gate_permutation if self.cell_type=='lstm' else lambda x:x
        result.update(Wx=change(convert(self.recurrent.weight_ih_l0.grad).T),Wh=change(convert(self.recurrent.weight_hh_l0.grad).T),b=change(convert(self.recurrent.bias_ih_l0.grad)))
        return result

class CourseAdam(torch.optim.Optimizer):
    """运行验证 Assignment 3 优化器的 alpha 与 epsilon 放置方式，不隐式换公式。"""
    def __init__(self,params,lr=5e-4):
        super().__init__(params,dict(lr=lr,beta1=.9,beta2=.999,eps=1e-8))
    @torch.no_grad()
    def step(self,closure=None):
        for group in self.param_groups:
            b1,b2=group['beta1'],group['beta2']
            for param in group['params']:
                if param.grad is None:continue
                state=self.state[param]
                if not state:state.update(t=0,m=torch.zeros_like(param),v=torch.zeros_like(param))
                state['t']+=1
                state['m'].mul_(b1).add_(param.grad,alpha=1-b1)
                state['v'].mul_(b2).addcmul_(param.grad,param.grad,value=1-b2)
                alpha=group['lr']*((1-b2**state['t'])**.5)/(1-b1**state['t'])
                param.addcdiv_(state['m'],state['v'].sqrt().add_(group['eps']),value=-alpha)

@isolated_random_state
def verify_equivalence(device='cpu'):
    """固定输入、权重、mask，检查损失、每个参数梯度和贪心解码。"""
    report={}
    for kind in ('rnn','lstm'):
        np.random.seed(231)
        vocabulary={'<NULL>':0,'<START>':1,'<END>':2,'cat':3,'dog':4}
        original=CaptioningRNN(vocabulary,input_dim=7,wordvec_dim=6,hidden_dim=8,cell_type=kind,dtype=np.float64)
        features=np.random.randn(3,7)
        captions=np.array([[1,3,4,2,0],[1,4,2,0,0],[1,3,3,4,2]],dtype=np.int64)
        expected_loss,expected_grads=original.loss(features,captions)
        model=TorchCaptioner(original).to(device)
        features_t=torch.tensor(features,device=device)
        captions_t=torch.tensor(captions,device=device)
        loss=model.loss(features_t,captions_t);loss.backward()
        np.testing.assert_allclose(loss.item(),expected_loss,rtol=1e-10,atol=1e-10)
        errors={}
        for key,value in model.numpy_gradients().items():
            np.testing.assert_allclose(value,expected_grads[key],rtol=1e-8,atol=1e-10)
            errors[key]=float(np.max(abs(value-expected_grads[key])))
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(model.sample(features_t,6).cpu().numpy(),original.sample(features,6))
        # 另外对照 optimizer 的多步状态更新，包含 epsilon 位置和 t 累加。
        from cs231n.optim import adam
        parameter=nn.Parameter(torch.tensor([.2,-.3],dtype=torch.float64,device=device));optimizer=CourseAdam([parameter])
        value=np.array([.2,-.3]);state={'learning_rate':5e-4}
        for gradient in (np.array([.1,-.2]),np.array([-.3,.4]),np.array([.05,.07])):
            parameter.grad=torch.tensor(gradient,device=device);optimizer.step();value,state=adam(value,gradient,state)
            np.testing.assert_allclose(parameter.detach().cpu().numpy(),value,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e-12)
        report[kind]={'loss_absolute_error':abs(loss.item()-expected_loss),'gradient_max_absolute_errors':errors,'greedy_decoding_equal':True,'adam_three_steps_equal':True,'device':device}
    return report

@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model,features,captions,image_indices,vocabulary,batch_size=100):
    """固定验证字幕的平均 loss 与单参考句 BLEU-1；不修改任何训练状态。"""
    model.eval();total=0.;bleus=[];examples=[]
    for start in range(0,len(captions),batch_size):
        targets=captions[start:start+batch_size];images=features[image_indices[start:start+batch_size]]
        total+=model.loss(images,targets).item()*len(targets)
        sampled=model.sample(images).cpu().numpy()
        predictions=decode_captions(sampled,vocabulary)
        references=decode_captions(targets.cpu().numpy(),vocabulary)
        for ref,hyp in zip(references,predictions):
            clean=lambda s:[w for w in s.split() if w not in ('<NULL>','<START>','<END>','<UNK>')]
            r,h=clean(ref),clean(hyp)
            score=float(sentence_bleu([r],h,weights=(1.0,))) if h else 0.
            bleus.append(score)
            if len(examples)<20:examples.append({'reference':ref,'prediction':hyp,'bleu1':score})
    return total/len(captions),float(np.mean(bleus)),examples

@isolated_random_state
def train_gpu(data,cell_type,epochs=20,batch_size=100,seed=231):
    """完整训练数据每轮无放回遍历，保存模型/优化器/RNG/排列/进度以恢复。"""
    if not torch.cuda.is_available():raise RuntimeError('完整 GPU 实验要求实际可用的 CUDA 设备')
    device='cuda';torch.set_num_threads(4)
    # 禁用 TF32，先保留与 NumPy float32 更直接的数值对应；不使用混合精度。
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=False;torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32=False
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic=True;torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark=False
    torch.manual_seed(seed);np.random.seed(seed);rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    original=CaptioningRNN(data['word_to_idx'],input_dim=512,wordvec_dim=256,hidden_dim=512,cell_type=cell_type,dtype=np.float32)
    model=TorchCaptioner(original).to(device)
    optimizer=CourseAdam(model.parameters(),lr=5e-4)
    folder=ROOT/'outputs'/f'full_coco_{cell_type}_gpu_equivalent'
    ckpt=folder/'checkpoints';ckpt.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    config={'backend':'PyTorch CUDA, equivalent course equations','cell_type':cell_type,'epochs':epochs,'batch_size':batch_size,'seed':seed,'wordvec_dim':256,'hidden_dim':512,'learning_rate':5e-4,'dtype':'float32','optimizer':'course Adam, original epsilon placement','train_captions':len(data['train_captions']),'validation':'fixed 1000 captions, seed 20261102; mean single-reference sentence BLEU-1'}
    (folder/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(config,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    features=torch.as_tensor(data['train_features'],device=device,dtype=torch.float32)
    captions=torch.as_tensor(data['train_captions'],device=device,dtype=torch.long)
    indices=torch.as_tensor(data['train_image_idxs'],device=device,dtype=torch.long)
    selected=np.random.RandomState(20261102).choice(len(data['val_captions']),1000,replace=False)
    vf=torch.as_tensor(data['val_features'],device=device,dtype=torch.float32)
    vc=torch.as_tensor(data['val_captions'][selected],device=device,dtype=torch.long)
    vi=torch.as_tensor(data['val_image_idxs'][selected],device=device,dtype=torch.long)
    n=len(captions);epoch=0;offset=0;step=0;order=rng.permutation(n);history=[];best_loss=float('inf');elapsed_prior=0.
    latest=ckpt/'latest.pt';best=ckpt/'best.pt'
    if latest.exists():
        state=torch.load(latest,map_location=device,weights_only=False)
        if state['config']!=config:raise ValueError('Resume config mismatch')
        model.load_state_dict(state['model']);optimizer.load_state_dict(state['optimizer'])
        epoch=state['epoch'];offset=state['offset'];step=state['step'];order=state['order'];history=state['history'];best_loss=state['best_loss'];elapsed_prior=state['elapsed_seconds'];rng.set_state(state['numpy_rng'])
        torch.set_rng_state(state['torch_rng'].cpu());torch.cuda.set_rng_state(state['cuda_rng'].cpu())
        if epoch==epochs and (folder/'metrics.json').exists():
            selected_model=torch.load(best,map_location=device,weights_only=True)
            model.load_state_dict(selected_model['model'])
            report=json.loads((folder/'metrics.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            # 已完成实验只加载结果；保留原训练耗时，不把阅读记录算作新训练。
            return model,report,history
    started=time.perf_counter()
    def elapsed():return elapsed_prior+time.perf_counter()-started
    def save():
        state={'config':config,'model':model.state_dict(),'optimizer':optimizer.state_dict(),'epoch':epoch,'offset':offset,'step':step,'order':order,'history':history,'best_loss':best_loss,'elapsed_seconds':elapsed(),'numpy_rng':rng.get_state(),'torch_rng':torch.get_rng_state(),'cuda_rng':torch.cuda.get_rng_state()}
        temporary=latest.with_suffix('.tmp');torch.save(state,temporary);os.replace(temporary,latest)
    def emit(record):
        record={**record,'elapsed_seconds':elapsed()}
        with (folder/'training.jsonl').open('a',encoding='utf-8') as f:f.write(json.dumps(record)+'\n')
        print(json.dumps(record),flush=True)
    emit({'event':'resume' if step else 'start','epoch':epoch,'step':step,'gpu':torch.cuda.get_device_name()})
    while epoch<epochs:
        model.train()
        while offset<n:
            rows=torch.as_tensor(order[offset:offset+batch_size],device=device)
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            loss=model.loss(features[indices[rows]],captions[rows])
            if not torch.isfinite(loss):save();raise FloatingPointError('nonfinite training loss')
            loss.backward();optimizer.step();step+=1;offset+=len(rows)
            if step%200==0:emit({'event':'batch','epoch':epoch+1,'step':step,'offset':offset,'loss':loss.item()})
            if step%1000==0:save()
        val_loss,bleu,examples=evaluate(model,vf,vc,vi,data['idx_to_word'])
        epoch+=1
        record={'epoch':epoch,'train_loss_last_batch':loss.item(),'validation_loss':val_loss,'validation_sentence_bleu1':bleu,'step':step}
        history.append(record)
        if val_loss<best_loss:
            best_loss=val_loss;torch.save({'model':model.state_dict(),'epoch':epoch,'validation_loss':val_loss,'validation_sentence_bleu1':bleu},best)
        emit({'event':'epoch_complete',**record})
        (folder/'history.json').write_text(json.dumps(history,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
        (folder/'caption_examples.json').write_text(json.dumps(examples,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
        offset=0;order=rng.permutation(n);save()
    selected_model=torch.load(best,map_location=device,weights_only=True);model.load_state_dict(selected_model['model'])
    val_loss,bleu,examples=evaluate(model,vf,vc,vi,data['idx_to_word'])
    report={'status':'complete','backend':'equivalent GPU; original NumPy full training not claimed','epochs_completed':epoch,'steps_completed':step,'train_captions_per_epoch':n,'best_epoch':selected_model['epoch'],'validation_loss':val_loss,'validation_sentence_bleu1':bleu,'validation_captions_evaluated':1000,'elapsed_seconds':elapsed(),'peak_gpu_memory_bytes':torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()}
    (folder/'metrics.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    (folder/'caption_examples.json').write_text(json.dumps(examples,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    emit({'event':'complete',**report})
    return model,report,history
```

## 03_Assignment3/cs231n/im2col_cython.pyx

[源文件](03_Assignment3/cs231n/im2col_cython.pyx)

```cython
import numpy as np
cimport numpy as np
cimport cython

# DTYPE = np.float64
# ctypedef np.float64_t DTYPE_t

ctypedef fused DTYPE_t:
    np.float32_t
    np.float64_t

def im2col_cython(np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=4] x, int field_height,
                  int field_width, int padding, int stride):
    cdef int N = x.shape[0]
    cdef int C = x.shape[1]
    cdef int H = x.shape[2]
    cdef int W = x.shape[3]

    cdef int HH = (H + 2 * padding - field_height) / stride + 1
    cdef int WW = (W + 2 * padding - field_width) / stride + 1

    cdef int p = padding
    cdef np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=4] x_padded = np.pad(x,
            ((0, 0), (0, 0), (p, p), (p, p)), mode='constant')

    cdef np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=2] cols = np.zeros(
            (C * field_height * field_width, N * HH * WW),
            dtype=x.dtype)

    # Moving the inner loop to a C function with no bounds checking works, but does
    # not seem to help performance in any measurable way.

    im2col_cython_inner(cols, x_padded, N, C, H, W, HH, WW,
                        field_height, field_width, padding, stride)
    return cols


@cython.boundscheck(False)
cdef int im2col_cython_inner(np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=2] cols,
                             np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=4] x_padded,
                             int N, int C, int H, int W, int HH, int WW,
                             int field_height, int field_width, int padding, int stride) except? -1:
    cdef int c, ii, jj, row, yy, xx, i, col

    for c in range(C):
        for yy in range(HH):
            for xx in range(WW):
                for ii in range(field_height):
                    for jj in range(field_width):
                        row = c * field_width * field_height + ii * field_height + jj
                        for i in range(N):
                            col = yy * WW * N + xx * N + i
                            cols[row, col] = x_padded[i, c, stride * yy + ii, stride * xx + jj]



def col2im_cython(np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=2] cols, int N, int C, int H, int W,
                  int field_height, int field_width, int padding, int stride):
    cdef np.ndarray x = np.empty((N, C, H, W), dtype=cols.dtype)
    cdef int HH = (H + 2 * padding - field_height) / stride + 1
    cdef int WW = (W + 2 * padding - field_width) / stride + 1
    cdef np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=4] x_padded = np.zeros((N, C, H + 2 * padding, W + 2 * padding),
                                        dtype=cols.dtype)

    # Moving the inner loop to a C-function with no bounds checking improves
    # performance quite a bit for col2im.
    col2im_cython_inner(cols, x_padded, N, C, H, W, HH, WW,
                        field_height, field_width, padding, stride)
    if padding > 0:
        return x_padded[:, :, padding:-padding, padding:-padding]
    return x_padded


@cython.boundscheck(False)
cdef int col2im_cython_inner(np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=2] cols,
                             np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=4] x_padded,
                             int N, int C, int H, int W, int HH, int WW,
                             int field_height, int field_width, int padding, int stride) except? -1:
    cdef int c, ii, jj, row, yy, xx, i, col

    for c in range(C):
        for ii in range(field_height):
            for jj in range(field_width):
                row = c * field_width * field_height + ii * field_height + jj
                for yy in range(HH):
                    for xx in range(WW):
                        for i in range(N):
                            col = yy * WW * N + xx * N + i
                            x_padded[i, c, stride * yy + ii, stride * xx + jj] += cols[row, col]


@cython.boundscheck(False)
@cython.wraparound(False)
cdef col2im_6d_cython_inner(np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=6] cols,
                            np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=4] x_padded,
                            int N, int C, int H, int W, int HH, int WW,
                            int out_h, int out_w, int pad, int stride):

    cdef int c, hh, ww, n, h, w
    for n in range(N):
        for c in range(C):
            for hh in range(HH):
                for ww in range(WW):
                    for h in range(out_h):
                        for w in range(out_w):
                            x_padded[n, c, stride * h + hh, stride * w + ww] += cols[c, hh, ww, n, h, w]


def col2im_6d_cython(np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=6] cols, int N, int C, int H, int W,
        int HH, int WW, int pad, int stride):
    cdef np.ndarray x = np.empty((N, C, H, W), dtype=cols.dtype)
    cdef int out_h = (H + 2 * pad - HH) / stride + 1
    cdef int out_w = (W + 2 * pad - WW) / stride + 1
    cdef np.ndarray[DTYPE_t, ndim=4] x_padded = np.zeros((N, C, H + 2 * pad, W + 2 * pad),
                                                  dtype=cols.dtype)

    col2im_6d_cython_inner(cols, x_padded, N, C, H, W, HH, WW, out_h, out_w, pad, stride)

    if pad > 0:
        return x_padded[:, :, pad:-pad, pad:-pad]
    return x_padded
```

## study_runtime.py

[源文件](study_runtime.py)

```python
"""课程共享路径、MNIST 只读加载和旧图像接口的显式兼容实现。"""
from pathlib import Path
import gzip, json, os, struct
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent

def data_path(key):
    """读取本机路径覆盖；未设置时使用项目旁共享目录，不触发网络下载。"""
    config=ROOT/'data_paths.local.json'
    paths=json.loads(config.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) if config.exists() else {}
    defaults={'cifar10':ROOT.parent/'datasets/cifar-10-batches-py','coco':ROOT.parent/'datasets/coco_captioning','imagenet_val':ROOT.parent/'datasets/imagenet_val_25.npz','shared':ROOT.parent/'datasets','mnist_gzip':ROOT.parent/'datasets/mnist'}
    return Path(os.environ.get('CS231N_'+key.upper(),paths.get(key,defaults[key])))

def imread(path):
    """文件/文件流 → Pillow 解码 → 独立像素数组；不修改源图片。"""
    with Image.open(path) as image:
        return np.array(image)

def imresize(array, size):
    """运行验证课程 uint8 图片的双线性缩放；tuple 按 (高度, 宽度) 解释。"""
    image=Image.fromarray(array)
    target=(int(image.width*size),int(image.height*size)) if np.isscalar(size) else (int(size[1]),int(size[0]))
    return np.array(image.resize(target,Image.Resampling.BILINEAR))

class ExistingMNIST:
    """直接解压已有 gzip 到内存，遵守 torchvision Dataset 的取样接口。"""
    def __init__(self, root=None, train=True, download=False, transform=None):
        prefix='train' if train else 't10k'
        directory=data_path('mnist_gzip')
        with gzip.open(directory/f'{prefix}-images-idx3-ubyte.gz','rb') as stream:
            magic,n,h,w=struct.unpack('>IIII',stream.read(16))
            assert magic==2051
            self.data=np.frombuffer(stream.read(),dtype=np.uint8).reshape(n,h,w)
        with gzip.open(directory/f'{prefix}-labels-idx1-ubyte.gz','rb') as stream:
            magic,n=struct.unpack('>II',stream.read(8)); assert magic==2049
            self.targets=np.frombuffer(stream.read(),dtype=np.uint8)
        assert len(self.data)==len(self.targets)==n
        self.transform=transform
    def __len__(self): return len(self.targets)
    def __getitem__(self,index):
        image=Image.fromarray(self.data[index])
        return (self.transform(image) if self.transform else image),int(self.targets[index])

def save_experiment(namespace, notebook_name):
    """保存当前实验的实际标量、曲线及已训练模型；原始数据不写入检查点。"""
    import pickle
    folder=ROOT/'outputs'/notebook_name
    checkpoints=folder/'checkpoints'
    checkpoints.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    scalars={}
    for key in ('accuracy','best_k','best_val','test_accuracy','test_acc','val_acc','acc_tr','acc_val'):
        value=namespace.get(key)
        if isinstance(value,(int,float,np.integer,np.floating)):
            scalars[key]=float(value)
    for key in ('best_net','best_model','best_svm','best_softmax','small_rnn_model','small_lstm_model','model','net','G','D','G_LS','D_LS','G_DC','D_DC'):
        value=namespace.get(key)
        if value is None: continue
        if hasattr(value,'params'):
            with (checkpoints/f'{key}.pkl').open('wb') as stream: pickle.dump(value,stream)
        elif hasattr(value,'W'):
            np.savez(checkpoints/f'{key}.npz',W=value.W)
        elif hasattr(value,'state_dict'):
            import torch
            torch.save(value.state_dict(),checkpoints/f'{key}.pt')
    curves={}
    for key in ('loss_hist','losses','accs','stats','results','k_to_accuracies'):
        value=namespace.get(key)
        if value is not None: curves[key]=value
    with (checkpoints/'experiment_state.pkl').open('wb') as stream: pickle.dump(curves,stream)
    (folder/'metrics.json').write_text(json.dumps(scalars,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print('本机实验指标：',scalars)
    return scalars
```

```python
"""将课程任务与验收边界置于每份作业和每个学习入口的最前面。"""
from pathlib import Path
import json
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
TASKS={
'001':('kNN 分类器','实现双循环、单循环和无循环 L2 距离；验证数值一致；实现多数投票；完成十折交叉验证选 k；绘制准确率与计时对照。','原题使用 5000 个训练样本和 500 个测试样本，约 27%～28% 是参考水平，保留实际结果。'),
'002':('多类线性 SVM','实现循环和向量化 hinge 损失及梯度；执行数值梯度检查；mini-batch SGD 训练；仅用验证集调参；测试与可视化分类权重；回答不可导点与权重解释问题。','训练/验证划分 49000/1000，测试按本 notebook 的 1000 张子集评估。'),
'003':('Softmax 分类器','实现稳定的循环和向量化交叉熵；检查梯度与计时；验证集选择学习率和正则化；报告测试结果并可视化权重；解释随机初始化损失。','验证准确率约 35% 为原题参考目标；不是通过改用测试集挑参来达到。'),
'004':('两层神经网络','手写 affine→ReLU→affine→Softmax 前后向；通过固定输出、损失和梯度检查；完成玩具数据拟合与 CIFAR-10 训练；分析曲线、调整超参数并测试最佳模型。','原题目标为验证和测试准确率超过 48%；超过 52% 为进一步挑战。'),
'005':('图像特征','提取 HOG 与 HSV 颜色直方图；用训练统计标准化；完整训练特征 SVM 和两层网络；验证调参；测试并展示误分类样例。','特征 SVM 验证约 44% 为参考；特征网络测试目标超过 55%。'),
'006':('模块化全连接网络与优化器','实现 affine、ReLU、SVM/Softmax 层及组合；检查两层、多层网络梯度；三层和五层网络过拟合 50 张图；实现 SGD、Momentum、RMSProp、Adam 并对照；训练最佳全连接模型。','两层及最终全连接模型验证准确率至少 50%；小样本过拟合实验需能达到 100% 训练准确率。'),
'007':('Batch Normalization','实现训练/推理前向和反向；检查均值方差、滑动统计和梯度；比较普通反向与简化反向；训练有/无 BN 的深层网络并完成初始化尺度扫描。','比较收敛、泛化和初始化敏感性，不预设 BN 必然提高所有配置的验证准确率。'),
'008':('Dropout','实现 inverted dropout 的训练/推理和反向；验证均值、置零比例与梯度；接入多层网络；完成 500 张图、25 轮的有/无 dropout 对照并解释结果。','本实现 p 为保留概率；dropout=0 表示关闭。所有对照均保留真实结果。'),
'009':('卷积网络','实现卷积/最大池化前后向与组合层；比较快速实现；检查三层卷积网损失和梯度；完成 100 样本过拟合与完整训练划分的一轮训练；显示卷积核；实现空间 BN。','一轮三层卷积训练的训练准确率应超过 40%；数值检查需区分绝对误差与接近零梯度的相对误差。'),
'010':('PyTorch 框架实践','分别使用原始张量、nn.Module、nn.Sequential 实现并训练两层网络和三层卷积网；完成开放式 CIFAR-10 模型训练；仅用验证集选模型；最后一次测试。','开放题最多 10 个 epoch，验证准确率至少 70%；完整测试集 10000 张。新增轻量方法使用独立模型并保持十轮预算。'),
'011':('RNN 图像字幕','实现单步/序列 RNN、词嵌入、时序仿射和带掩码 Softmax；数值检查完整模型；50 字幕小样本训练 50 轮；实现正确自回归解码并展示图文。','新增完整数据训练覆盖每轮全部 400135 条课程字幕，共 20 轮；经对照验证的 GPU 等价后端执行，原 NumPy 教学实验独立保留。'),
'012':('LSTM 图像字幕','实现 LSTM 门控、单步/序列前后向；固定参考和数值梯度检查；50 字幕小样本训练 50 轮；生成字幕并按原题 BLEU-1 评估。','新增完整数据 GPU 等价训练共 20 轮。固定 1000 条验证字幕的平均单句 BLEU-1 单独报告；0.3 是原题加分参考，不等同于 corpus BLEU-4。'),
'013':('网络可视化','冻结预训练 SqueezeNet；计算并展示显著图；用归一化输入梯度生成目标类别欺骗图并断言成功；完成带 L2、抖动和模糊的类别可视化。','使用课程 ImageNet 验证样例。网络参数不训练；实际优化和求导对象为输入图像。'),
'014':('风格迁移与特征反演','实现并数值检查内容、Gram、风格和 TV 损失；完成三组指定风格迁移；执行随机初始化的特征反演；保存中间与最终图像。','各组按原题固定参数执行 200 次像素优化，预训练骨干不重新训练。'),
'015':('生成对抗网络','完成噪声、判别器、生成器、稳定 BCE、LSGAN 损失及结构检查；训练普通 GAN、LSGAN、DCGAN；保存样本并回答比较问题。','普通 GAN 与 LSGAN 各 10 轮，DCGAN 5 轮；复用已有 MNIST。原循环按训练采样器取 50000 张，跳过不足 128 张的尾批，每轮实际 49920 张。')}

def main():
    for group in sorted(ROOT.glob('*_Assignment*')):
        entries=[]
        for path in sorted(group.glob('[0-9][0-9][0-9]_*.ipynb')):
            number=path.name[:3];title,requirements,boundary=TASKS[number]
            nb=json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            header=f'# {number} {title}：题目要求\n\n**需要完成：** {requirements}\n\n**实验范围与验收：** {boundary}\n\n**阅读顺序：** 先阅读原题和代码块前的中文解析，再检查实际输出与问题记录；若本题包含方法二，它紧随对应方法一，使用独立状态。方法一不得依赖方法二才能运行。\n\n**来源与版本：** 以指定初始版本仓库的 notebook 为本次题目依据，包含 2016 年基础题和仓库后加的 PyTorch 题，不冒称单一年度原版。初始版本参考：https://cs231n.github.io/ 。\n'
            nb['cells']=[c for c in nb['cells'] if c.get('id')!='assignment-requirements']
            nb['cells'].insert(0,{'cell_type':'markdown','metadata':{},'id':'assignment-requirements','source':header.splitlines(keepends=True)})
            path.write_text(json.dumps(nb,ensure_ascii=False,indent=1)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
            entries.append(f'## {number}. {title}\n\n{requirements}\n\n{boundary}\n\n[进入完整实验与解析]({path.name})\n')
        text=f'# {group.name}：题目要求与学习顺序\n\n本作业使用 `cs231n_zx_test` 环境及同名内核。共享数据通过项目路径配置复用，不重复下载；TensorFlow 版本只作历史参考，不属于本次执行范围。\n\n'+'\n'.join(entries)
        (group/'000_作业要求.md').write_text(text,encoding='utf-8')
    (ROOT/'assignment_requirements.json').write_text(json.dumps(TASKS,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
if __name__=='__main__':main()
```

## tools/audit_delivery.py

[源文件](tools/audit_delivery.py)

```python
"""交付一致性检查：notebook、执行证据、题目、解析、链接、图像与训练预算。"""
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,re,subprocess,sys
from urllib.parse import unquote
import nbformat
import mistune
from PIL import Image
from jupyter_client.kernelspec import KernelSpecManager
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def code_hash(notebook):
    blocks=[(c.get('id'),''.join(c['source'])) for c in notebook['cells'] if c['cell_type']=='code' and ''.join(c['source']).strip()]
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(blocks,ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()

def markdown_targets(text):
    # 使用真正的 Markdown 解析器；三反引号行内代码不能误当成未闭合围栏。
    tree=mistune.create_markdown(renderer='ast')(text)
    targets=[]
    def walk(items):
        for item in items:
            if item['type'] in ('link','image'):
                targets.append(item.get('attrs',{}).get('url',''))
            elif item['type'] in ('inline_html','block_html'):
                targets.extend(re.findall(r'<img[^>]+src=["\']([^"\']+)',item.get('raw','')))
            walk(item.get('children',[]))
    walk(tree)
    return targets

def main():
    failures=[];notebooks=[];execution={}
    old_manifest_path=ROOT/'outputs/execution_manifest.json'
    old_manifest=json.loads(old_manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) if old_manifest_path.exists() else {}
    paths=sorted(ROOT.glob('*_Assignment*/[0-9][0-9][0-9]_*.ipynb'))
    if len(paths)!=15:failures.append(f'Expected 15 notebooks, found {len(paths)}')
    for path in paths:
        notebook=nbformat.read(path,as_version=4);nbformat.validate(notebook)
        code_cells=[c for c in notebook.cells if c.cell_type=='code' and c.source.strip()]
        errors=[o for c in code_cells for o in c.outputs if o.output_type=='error']
        if errors:failures.append(f'{path.name}: error outputs')
        if notebook.cells[0].get('id')!='assignment-requirements':failures.append(f'{path.name}: missing opening requirements')
        if notebook.metadata.kernelspec.name!='cs231n_zx_test' or notebook.metadata.kernelspec.display_name!='cs231n_zx_test':failures.append(f'{path.name}: incorrect kernel')
        missing=[];unexecuted=[]
        for index,cell in enumerate(notebook.cells):
            if cell.cell_type!='code' or not cell.source.strip():continue
            if index==0 or notebook.cells[index-1].cell_type!='markdown':missing.append(cell.id)
            if cell.execution_count is None:unexecuted.append(cell.id)
        if missing:failures.append(f'{path.name}: missing preceding explanation {missing}')
        if unexecuted:failures.append(f'{path.name}: unexecuted {unexecuted}')
        for cell in notebook.cells:
            if cell.cell_type=='markdown' and re.search(r'TODO:\s*(?:YOUR ANSWER|Describe)|fill this in|fill in this',cell.source,re.I):failures.append(f'{path.name}: unanswered prompt')
            if '\ufffd' in cell.source:failures.append(f'{path.name}: replacement character in source')
        actual_hash=code_hash(notebook)
        runs=sorted((ROOT/'outputs'/path.stem).glob('run-*.ipynb'))
        successful=[]
        for run in runs:
            log=run.with_suffix('.jsonl')
            if not log.exists():continue
            lines=log.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()
            if lines and json.loads(lines[-1]).get('event')=='complete':successful.append(run)
        if successful:
            latest=successful[-1];executed=nbformat.read(latest,as_version=4)
            if code_hash(executed)!=actual_hash:failures.append(f'{path.name}: code differs from latest complete run')
            execution[path.stem]={'code_sha256':actual_hash,'completed_run':latest.stem,'code_cells':len(code_cells),'error_outputs':len(errors)}
        elif path.stem in old_manifest:
            execution[path.stem]=old_manifest[path.stem]
            if execution[path.stem]['code_sha256']!=actual_hash:failures.append(f'{path.name}: code differs from recorded execution')
        else:failures.append(f'{path.name}: no complete execution evidence')
        notebooks.append({'notebook':path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),'code_cells':len(code_cells),'preceding_explanation_missing':missing,'unexecuted':unexecuted,'error_outputs':len(errors)})
    old_manifest_path.write_text(json.dumps(execution,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    mapping=json.loads((ROOT/'file_mapping.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8')) if (ROOT/'file_mapping.json').exists() else []
    if mapping and len(mapping)!=150:failures.append('Incomplete local file inventory')
    for record in mapping:
        if record.get('status')=='active' and not (ROOT/record['destination']).is_file():
            failures.append('Missing active mapped file: '+record['destination'])
        # archived_local 的条目不随 Git 发布；在线通过固定初始版本提交追溯。
    # 只检查交付文本，源代码围栏中的示例链接不当作实际文档依赖。
    missing_links=[]
    documents=list(ROOT.glob('*.md'))+list(ROOT.glob('*_Assignment*/*.md'))
    for path in documents:
        for target in markdown_targets(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')):
            if re.match(r'^(https?://|mailto:|#|data:)',target):continue
            target=unquote(target.strip('<>').split('#')[0])
            if target and not (path.parent/target).exists():missing_links.append({'document':path.name,'target':target})
    if missing_links:failures.append(f'{len(missing_links)} broken local Markdown links')
    image_count=0
    for image in (ROOT/'assets/complete_record').rglob('*.png'):
        with Image.open(image) as img:img.verify()
        image_count+=1
    budgets={}
    for kind in ('rnn','lstm'):
        path=ROOT/'outputs'/f'full_coco_{kind}_gpu_equivalent/metrics.json'
        result=json.loads(path.read_text())
        passed=result['status']=='complete' and result['epochs_completed']==20 and result['steps_completed']==80040 and result['train_captions_per_epoch']==400135
        if not passed:failures.append(f'{kind}: incomplete full-data training')
        budgets[kind]={'epochs':20,'updates':80040,'passed':passed}
    for name,epochs,steps in [('vanilla',10,3900),('lsgan',10,3900),('dcgan',5,1950)]:
        result=json.loads((ROOT/f'outputs/015_GANs_PyTorch/{name}_report.json').read_text())
        if result['epochs_completed']!=epochs or result['steps_completed']!=steps:failures.append(name+': incomplete training')
        budgets[name]={'epochs':epochs,'updates':steps}
    extension=json.loads((ROOT/'outputs/010_PyTorch/augmentation_extension/metrics.json').read_text())
    if extension['status']!='complete' or extension['additional_epochs']!=30:failures.append('CIFAR extension incomplete')
    kernel=KernelSpecManager().get_kernel_spec('cs231n_zx_test')
    kernel_ok=Path(kernel.argv[0]).resolve()==Path(sys.executable).resolve() and kernel.display_name=='cs231n_zx_test'
    if not kernel_ok:failures.append('Jupyter kernel executable mismatch')
    baseline=ROOT.parent/'base'
    baseline_clean=None
    if (baseline/'.git').exists():
        baseline_clean=not subprocess.check_output(['git','status','--porcelain'],cwd=baseline).strip()
        if not baseline_clean:failures.append('Reference base has modifications')
    report={'status':'passed' if not failures else 'failed','notebooks':notebooks,'total_nonempty_code_cells':sum(x['code_cells'] for x in notebooks),'images_verified':image_count,'kernel_executable_matches_environment':kernel_ok,'training_budgets':budgets,'extended_cifar_epochs':extension['total_epoch_budget'],'missing_links':missing_links,'failures':failures,'evidence_boundary':'This audit checks delivered executed artifacts and recorded complete runs; it does not rerun training.'}
    (ROOT/'outputs/delivery_audit.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k not in ('notebooks','training_budgets')},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
    if failures:raise SystemExit(1)

if __name__=='__main__':main()
```
