## tools/configure_data.py

[源文件](tools/configure_data.py)

```python
"""验证现有数据并写本机路径；不复制、不修改已有数据。"""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse, hashlib, json, sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def md5(path):
    digest = hashlib.md5()
    with path.open('rb') as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()

def main():
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
    from study_runtime import data_path
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--cifar10', type=Path, default=data_path('cifar10'))
    parser.add_argument('--mnist', type=Path, default=data_path('mnist_gzip'))
    parser.add_argument('--shared', type=Path, default=data_path('shared'))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    # 输入为本机发现的数据目录；逐批校验官方 CIFAR-10 校验值后才注册路径。
    cifar = args.cifar10.resolve()
    checks = {'data_batch_1':'c99cafc152244af753f735de768cd75f','data_batch_2':'d4bba439e000b95fd0a9bffe97cbabec','data_batch_3':'54ebc095f3ab1f0389bbae665268c751','data_batch_4':'634d18415352ddfa80567beed471001a','data_batch_5':'482c414d41f54cd18b22e5b47cb7c3cb','test_batch':'40351d587109b95175f43aff81a1287e'}
    verified = {name: md5(cifar/name) for name in checks}
    assert verified == checks, 'CIFAR-10 checksum mismatch'
    mnist=args.mnist.resolve()
    mnist_checks={'train-images-idx3-ubyte.gz':'f68b3c2dcbeaaa9fbdd348bbdeb94873','train-labels-idx1-ubyte.gz':'d53e105ee54ea40749a09fcbcd1e9432','t10k-images-idx3-ubyte.gz':'9fb629c4189551a2d022fa330f9573f3','t10k-labels-idx1-ubyte.gz':'ec29112dd5afa0611ce80d1b7f02629c'}
    assert {name:md5(mnist/name) for name in mnist_checks}==mnist_checks
    shared=args.shared.resolve()
    shared.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    config={'cifar10':str(cifar),'mnist_gzip':str(mnist),'coco':str(shared/'coco_captioning'),'imagenet_val':str(shared/'imagenet_val_25.npz'),'shared':str(shared)}
    (ROOT/'data_paths.local.json').write_text(json.dumps(config,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    report={'cifar10':{'status':'verified_existing','md5':verified},'mnist':{'status':'verified_existing','md5':mnist_checks},'coco':{'status':'present' if (shared/'coco_captioning/coco2014_captions.h5').exists() else 'missing'},'imagenet_val':{'status':'present' if (shared/'imagenet_val_25.npz').exists() else 'missing'}}
    (ROOT/'outputs').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (ROOT/'outputs/data_inventory.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))

if __name__=='__main__': main()
```

## tools/export_complete_markdown.py

[源文件](tools/export_complete_markdown.py)

```python
"""把三份作业的完整 notebook 内容、输出图像及核心源码合并为 Markdown。"""
from pathlib import Path
import base64, hashlib, json, re
from urllib.parse import quote
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEST=ROOT/'002_三份作业完整学习记录.md'
ASSETS=ROOT/'assets'/'complete_record'

def fence(text,language=''):
    longest=max([len(x) for x in re.findall(r'`+',text)]+[2])
    marker='`'*max(3,longest+1)
    return f'{marker}{language}\n{text.rstrip()}\n{marker}\n'

def clean_console(text):
    return re.sub(r'\x1b\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z]','',text)

def rebase_links(text,directory):
    def replace(match):
        target=match.group(2)
        if re.match(r'^(https?://|mailto:|#|data:)',target):return match.group(0)
        raw=target.strip('<>')
        if ' ' in raw and not (directory/raw).exists():return match.group(0)
        path=(directory/raw).resolve()
        if not path.is_relative_to(ROOT):return match.group(0)
        return match.group(1)+quote(path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),safe='/#')+')'
    return re.sub(r'(!?\[[^\]]*\]\()([^\)]+)\)',replace,text)

def notebook_markdown(path):
    notebook=json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'));lines=[f'## {path.stem}\n',f'[可执行 notebook]({quote(path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix())})\n']
    folder=ASSETS/path.stem;folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    counts={'markdown_cells':0,'code_cells':0,'images':0,'error_outputs':0}
    for index,cell in enumerate(notebook['cells']):
        source=''.join(cell.get('source',[]))
        if cell['cell_type']=='markdown':
            counts['markdown_cells']+=1
            lines.append(rebase_links(source,path.parent)+'\n')
        elif cell['cell_type']=='code':
            if not source.strip():continue
            counts['code_cells']+=1;lines.append(fence(source,'python'))
            pending_stream=[]
            def flush_stream():
                if pending_stream:
                    text=clean_console(''.join(pending_stream))
                    if text.strip():lines.append(fence(text,'text'))
                    pending_stream.clear()
            for j,output in enumerate(cell.get('outputs',[])):
                kind=output.get('output_type')
                if kind=='stream':
                    pending_stream.append(''.join(output.get('text',[])))
                    continue
                elif kind=='error':
                    flush_stream()
                    counts['error_outputs']+=1
                    lines.append(fence(clean_console('\n'.join(output.get('traceback',[]))),'text'))
                elif kind in ('display_data','execute_result'):
                    flush_stream()
                    data=output.get('data',{})
                    if 'image/png' in data:
                        image=folder/f"{cell.get('id',str(index))}-{j:02d}.png"
                        image.write_bytes(base64.b64decode(''.join(data['image/png'])))
                        lines.append(f'![{path.stem}，单元 {cell.get("id",index)} 的实际输出]({image.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()})\n');counts['images']+=1
                    elif 'image/svg+xml' in data:
                        image=folder/f"{cell.get('id',str(index))}-{j:02d}.svg"
                        image.write_text(''.join(data['image/svg+xml']),encoding='utf-8')
                        lines.append(f'![实际输出]({image.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()})\n');counts['images']+=1
                    elif 'text/plain' in data:lines.append(fence(''.join(data['text/plain']),'text'))
            flush_stream()
    return '\n'.join(lines),counts

def main():
    ASSETS.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    lines=['# CS231n：三份作业完整学习记录\n',
           '本文件按作业顺序汇总题目要求、原笔记、逐块中文解析、完整代码、本机运行输出与改进实验。图像以相对路径保存在 `assets/complete_record`；阅读或移动本文件时需同时保留资源目录。底层课程源码列在文末附录，便于把 notebook 调用和实际实现对应起来。\n',
           '[总实现流程](000_总实现流程.md) · [文件索引](001_文件索引.md) · [问题与解决记录](003_问题与解决记录.md)\n']
    manifest={}
    for group in sorted(ROOT.glob('*_Assignment*')):
        lines.append('\n---\n')
        lines.append(rebase_links((group/'000_作业要求.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8'),group))
        for notebook in sorted(group.glob('[0-9][0-9][0-9]_*.ipynb')):
            text,counts=notebook_markdown(notebook);lines.append(text);manifest[notebook.stem]=counts
    lines.append('\n# 附录：底层实现与运行工具\n\n以下按作业保持独立的 `cs231n` 包；同名模块不代表可以跨作业混用。TensorFlow 参考和历史二进制不属于本次执行源码。\n')
    sources=[]
    for group in sorted(ROOT.glob('*_Assignment*')):
        sources.extend(sorted((group/'cs231n').rglob('*.py')))
        sources.extend(sorted((group/'cs231n').rglob('*.pyx')))
    sources.extend([ROOT/'study_runtime.py'])
    sources.extend(sorted((ROOT/'tools').glob('*.py')))
    for source in sources:
        if source.name in ('squeezenet.py','archive_auxiliary.py','full_captioning.py','train_full_coco.py') or 'archive' in source.parts:continue
        relative=source.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
        lines.append(f'## {relative}\n\n[源文件]({quote(relative)})\n')
        lines.append(fence(source.read_text(encoding='utf-8'),'cython' if source.suffix=='.pyx' else 'python'))
    # 汇总文档清理行尾空格，不修改源 notebook 或 Python 文件。
    rendered='\n'.join(line.rstrip() for line in '\n'.join(lines).splitlines())+'\n'
    DEST.write_text(rendered,encoding='utf-8')
    manifest['_source_files']=len(sources)
    manifest['_markdown_bytes']=DEST.stat().st_size
    (ROOT/'outputs/markdown_export_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps({'notebooks':len(manifest)-2,'images':sum(v.get('images',0) for v in manifest.values() if isinstance(v,dict)),'bytes':DEST.stat().st_size},ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=='__main__':main()
```

## tools/run_notebook.py

[源文件](tools/run_notebook.py)

```python
"""干净内核运行，逐单元保存输出；失败输出与成功结果明确区分。"""
import argparse, datetime, json, os, pathlib, sys, time, traceback
import nbformat
from nbclient import NotebookClient

ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('number',help='Three-digit notebook number')
    parser.add_argument('--original-only',action='store_true',help='跳过方法二，并保留完整源 notebook 不变')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    matches=list(ROOT.glob(f'*_Assignment*/{args.number}_*.ipynb'))
    if len(matches)!=1: raise ValueError(matches)
    source=matches[0]
    out=ROOT/'outputs'/source.stem
    out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    stamp=datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')
    prefix='original-only-run' if args.original_only else 'run'
    result=out/f'{prefix}-{stamp}.ipynb'
    logpath=out/f'{prefix}-{stamp}.jsonl'
    nb=nbformat.read(source,as_version=4)
    if args.original_only:
        nb.cells=[cell for cell in nb.cells if 'method2' not in cell.get('metadata',{}).get('tags',[])]
    for cell in nb.cells:
        if cell.cell_type=='code': cell.outputs=[];cell.execution_count=None
    started=time.time()
    def log(event,**fields):
        record={'event':event,'time':datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),'elapsed_s':time.time()-started,**fields}
        with logpath.open('a',encoding='utf-8') as stream: stream.write(json.dumps(record,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
        print(json.dumps(record,ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)
    def on_start(cell,cell_index,**kwargs): log('cell_start',index=cell_index,id=cell.get('id'))
    def on_executed(cell,cell_index,**kwargs):
        nbformat.write(nb,result)
        log('cell_executed',index=cell_index,id=cell.get('id'))
    client=NotebookClient(nb,kernel_name='cs231n_zx_test',timeout=None,allow_errors=False,resources={'metadata':{'path':str(source.parent)}},on_cell_start=on_start,on_cell_executed=on_executed)
    log('start',source=str(source.relative_to(ROOT)),python=sys.executable,scope='original_only' if args.original_only else 'full')
    try:
        client.execute()
        nbformat.write(nb,result)
        if not args.original_only:
            nbformat.write(nb,source)
        log('complete',artifact=str(result.relative_to(ROOT)))
    except Exception as exc:
        nbformat.write(nb,result)
        log('failed',error=str(exc),traceback=traceback.format_exc())
        raise

if __name__=='__main__': main()
```

## tools/verify_core.py

[源文件](tools/verify_core.py)

```python
"""独立验证关键修复：框架自动求导作为手写层的交叉检查。"""
from pathlib import Path
import json,sys
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from threadpoolctl import threadpool_limits
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'02_Assignment2'))
from cs231n.optim import adam
from cs231n.classifiers.cnn import ThreeLayerConvNet
from cs231n.classifiers.fc_net import FullyConnectedNet

def tensor(value):return torch.tensor(value,dtype=torch.float64,requires_grad=True)

def compare_gradients(model,values,labels,forward):
    expected_loss,expected=model.loss(values,labels)
    params={k:tensor(v) for k,v in model.params.items()}
    x=torch.tensor(values,dtype=torch.float64);y=torch.tensor(labels,dtype=torch.long)
    loss=F.cross_entropy(forward(x,params),y)
    for key,value in params.items():
        if key.startswith('W'):loss=loss+.5*model.reg*(value**2).sum()
    loss.backward()
    np.testing.assert_allclose(loss.item(),expected_loss,atol=1e-10,rtol=1e-10)
    errors={}
    for key,value in params.items():
        actual=value.grad.numpy()
        np.testing.assert_allclose(actual,expected[key],atol=1e-9,rtol=1e-7)
        errors[key]={'max_absolute_error':float(np.max(abs(actual-expected[key]))),'gradient_max_absolute_value':float(np.max(abs(expected[key])))}
    return {'loss_absolute_error':abs(loss.item()-expected_loss),'gradients':errors}

def main():
    np.random.seed(231);torch.set_num_threads(2)
    # 不依赖四舍五入到八位小数的参考表，直接对照双精度多步 Adam。
    weights=np.array([.2,-.3,.04]);configuration={};p=tensor(weights);optimizer=torch.optim.Adam([p],lr=1e-3)
    for gradient in (np.array([.1,-.2,.3]),np.array([-.3,.4,-.1]),np.array([.05,.07,.09])):
        weights,configuration=adam(weights,gradient,configuration)
        p.grad=torch.tensor(gradient);optimizer.step()
        np.testing.assert_allclose(weights,p.detach().numpy(),atol=1e-12,rtol=1e-12)
    assert configuration['t']==3
    # 运行验证原卷积 notebook 梯度检查的实际初始化与尺寸，定位相对误差。
    np.random.seed(231)
    x=np.random.randn(2,3,16,16);y=np.random.randint(10,size=2)
    cnn=ThreeLayerConvNet(input_dim=(3,16,16),num_filters=3,filter_size=3,hidden_dim=7,num_classes=10,dtype=np.float64,reg=0.)
    def cnn_forward(x,p):
        x=F.max_pool2d(F.relu(F.conv2d(x,p['W1'],p['b1'],padding=1)),2)
        return F.relu(x.flatten(1)@p['W2']+p['b2'])@p['W3']+p['b3']
    cnn_result=compare_gradients(cnn,x,y,cnn_forward)
    fc=FullyConnectedNet([20,30],input_dim=15,num_classes=10,use_batchnorm=True,dtype=np.float64,weight_scale=.05,reg=.1)
    x=np.random.randn(4,15);y=np.array([1,2,3,4])
    def bn_forward(x,p):
        for i in (1,2):
            x=x@p[f'W{i}']+p[f'b{i}']
            x=(x-x.mean(dim=0))/torch.sqrt(x.var(dim=0,unbiased=False)+1e-5)
            x=F.relu(x*p[f'gamma{i}']+p[f'beta{i}'])
        return x@p['W3']+p['b3']
    bn_result=compare_gradients(fc,x,y,bn_forward)
    report={'adam_three_step_torch_agreement':True,'cnn_autograd_comparison':cnn_result,'batchnorm_network_autograd_comparison':bn_result}
    (ROOT/'outputs/core_verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))

if __name__=='__main__':
    with threadpool_limits(limits=2):main()
```

## tools/verify_style_losses.py

[源文件](tools/verify_style_losses.py)

```python
"""直接提取当前 notebook 的四个实际损失函数，用双精度数值梯度检查。"""
from pathlib import Path
import json
import torch
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def main():
    path=ROOT/'03_Assignment3/014_StyleTransfer_PyTorch.ipynb'
    notebook=json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    namespace={'torch':torch}
    for cell in notebook['cells']:
        if cell.get('metadata',{}).get('original_cell_index') in (10,14,18,22):
            exec(''.join(cell['source']),namespace)
    torch.manual_seed(231)
    x=torch.randn(2,3,4,5,dtype=torch.float64,requires_grad=True)
    target=torch.randn_like(x)
    checks={}
    checks['content']=torch.autograd.gradcheck(lambda z:namespace['content_loss'](.1,z,target),(x,))
    checks['gram']=torch.autograd.gradcheck(namespace['gram_matrix'],(x,))
    checks['tv']=torch.autograd.gradcheck(lambda z:namespace['tv_loss'](z,.2),(x,))
    gram_target=namespace['gram_matrix'](target).detach()
    checks['style']=torch.autograd.gradcheck(lambda z:namespace['style_loss']([z],[0],[gram_target],[.3]),(x,))
    (ROOT/'outputs/style_loss_gradcheck.json').write_text(json.dumps(checks,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(checks)

if __name__=='__main__':main()
```
